| [`autumn-woods`](docs/scenes/autumn-woods.png) | nature, forest, showcase | realityhd | Autumn wood of red maple, gold aspen, beech and oak over leaf litter, a dirt path winding past logs, a snag and mossy rocks. |
| [`alpine-meadow`](docs/scenes/alpine-meadow.png) | nature, outdoor, water, rock | hunter | Alpine meadow below a limestone cliff band: scree, larch, fir and pine stands, pebble stream, mossy boulders, flowers. |
| [`canyon-road`](docs/scenes/canyon-road.png) | desert, road, outdoor, rock | hunter | Desert highway through a sandy canyon: sandstone buttes, dunes, saguaro, barrel cactus, agave, dry grass, rock piles. |
| [`winter-forest`](docs/scenes/winter-forest.png) | snow, forest, outdoor, nature | hunter | Snowy clearing ringed by snow-laden spruce and fir: drifts, snow-capped boulders, fallen log, firewood rick, chopping block. |

| [`executive-office`](docs/scenes/executive-office.png) | office, interior, showcase | realityhd | Corner executive office: walnut desk with open book, laptop and lit banker's lamp, bookcases, club chair, rug, golden sun through tall windows. |
| [`office-lobby`](docs/scenes/office-lobby.png) | office, interior, showcase | realityhd | Office lobby: glazed street front with glass door, terrazzo floor, marble reception desk, elevator bank, leather sofas and planters. |
| [`conference-room`](docs/scenes/conference-room.png) | office, interior, showcase | realityhd | Glass-walled conference room: boardroom table, eight mesh chairs, laptops and notes, wall display, whiteboard, window wall and glass door. |
| [`office-plaza`](docs/scenes/office-plaza.png) | urban, outdoor, showcase | realityhd | Plaza between two curtain-wall office blocks: paving, planters with maples, benches, bollards and lamps in late-afternoon sun. |

| [`hospital-lobby`](docs/scenes/hospital-lobby.png) | hospital, medical, interior, showcase | realityhd | ER waiting and intake lobby: glazed entrance, intake nurse station, check-in kiosks, beam seating rows, wheelchair, sanitizer stations and doors to treatment. |
| [`er-room`](docs/scenes/er-room.png) | hospital, medical, interior, showcase | realityhd | ER treatment room: stretcher under a lit headwall, vitals monitor, IV pump, exam table and stool, counter with diagnostic instruments, crash cart, privacy curtain. |
| [`operating-room`](docs/scenes/operating-room.png) | hospital, medical, interior, showcase | realityhd | Operating room: surgical table under a dual-head LED light, anesthesia boom and workstation, draped Mayo stand and back table with instruments, doors. |
| [`hospital`](docs/scenes/hospital.png) | hospital, medical, interior, showcase | realityhd | Full ER floor: waiting lobby, treatment corridor with scrub sink and parked equipment, ER treatment room and operating room, parked bed and oxygen, all doors and devices live. |
